みずほ経済 EXPRESS

みずほ GDP ナウ（2026 年 10 月中旬時点） 
～7～9 月期 GDP は前期比＋0.03％（年率＋0.10％）と推計～

調査部  シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山祐資 
同             エコノミスト  阿部大樹 
080-1069-4835 yusuke.koshiyama@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 10 月中旬時点の「みずほ GDP ナウ」は 7～9 月期 GDP を前期比＋0.10％と予測 
みずほ総合研究所では景気動向をタイムリーに把握するべく、浦沢（2023）等を参考にGDPナウキ
ャスティングに取り組んできた。これを「みずほGDPナウ」と呼称することとし、太田他（2024）で
はデータドリブン予測である「みずほGDPナウ」が民間予測平均並みの予測精度を確保できることを
示した。その上で、酒井他（2024）をはじめとして、月次経済指標を用いたGDPナウキャスティング
の結果を紹介してきたところである。 
本稿では10月中旬時点までに得られる月次経済指標を用い、 「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期GDP
の予測結果を紹介する。予測に用いたデータは、8月分の鉱工業生産、 消費活動指数、 所定外労働時間、
消費財出荷指数、第3次産業活動指数、9月分の中小企業景況調査（売上げ見通しDI）である1。図表1
には、予測に使用した各月次経済指標の推移を示している。 
図表 1 各月次経済指標の推移

（注）青字は改善、赤字は悪化を示す

日本銀行「消費活動指数」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 2

2

モデルによる10月中旬時点における7 ～9月期実質GDP成長率の予測値は、図表2のとおり前期比＋
0.03％ （年率＋0.10％）となった。 他機関による予測では、東京財団政策研究所による10月15日時点の
ナウキャスティングが同＋0.29％（年率＋1.18％）、日本経済研究センターが公表した10月のESPフォ
ーキャスト調査（回答期間：2026年9月30日～10月7日、回答者37名）における民間予測値平均が前期
比年率＋0.08％となっている。「みずほGDPナウ」の予測値は、民間コンセンサスよりやや高いもの
の、概ね近い伸びを予測していると言える。 
なお、多くの民間エコノミストが保守的な見方をしている理由の一つには、GDP統計作成上のテク
ニカルな要因がある。4～6月期には、 中東情勢悪化を背景に原油 ・ ナフサ輸入が大幅に減少したほか、
石油化学製品の供給途絶懸念から幅広い企業で在庫積み増しの動きが生じた。やや直感に反するが、
いずれもGDPに対しては押し上げ要因となる。 そのため、4～6月期GDPは前期比＋0.36％ （年率＋1.45％）
とプラス成長になったものの、 成長率が示すほど景気の実態は強くなかったとみるのが自然であろう。
反対に、7～9月期については、4～6月期の反動で輸入増・在庫減の動きが顕在化し、GDPには一定の
下方圧力がかかる可能性がある。みずほ総合研究所が9月に公表したレポート2でも、こうしたテクニ
カル要因を考慮の上、7～9月期GDPは横ばい圏の伸びにとどまると予想している。 
■ 「みずほ GDP ナウ」による予測値下方修正の主因は生産・消費関連指標の悪化 
「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期のGDP（1次速報値） の予測にあたっては10月中旬までに公表さ
れた月次経済指標を使って予測値を4回更新した。図表3では、予測値の更新過程と各月次経済指標の
寄与度を示している3。予測値が下方修正された主因は、8月の鉱工業生産、 消費財出荷指数の低下だ。
特に鉱工業生産指数については、7月末に発生した熊本地震による影響が下押し要因になったとみら
れる。 大手自動車メーカーで工場の操業が停止した影響で自動車が前月比▲6.8％と大幅に減産したほ
か、 電子部品 ・ デバイスも同▲1.9％の減産となった。7月調査時点の生産予測指数は前月比＋3.1％（ 補
図表 2 各機関の実質GDP成長率予測値 図表 3 予測値の改定過程と各経済指標の寄与度

キャスティング」、日本経済研究センター「ESPフォーキャス
ト調査」等より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成 
（注） モデル予測値は、 各時点で知り得るデータを用いて推計した実
質GDP成長率予測値。棒グラフは予測値に対する各経済指標の
寄与度を示す。「みずほGDPナウ」は各経済指標の結果を公表
前に予測しており （モデル予測値） 、 寄与度はモデル予測値と
実績値の乖離幅から計算される。 なお、 各経済指標の確報を受
けて指標が改定された場合も寄与度に反映される

### Page 3

3

正値ベース）と増産が見込まれていたが、大幅に下振れた格好だ。一方、世界的に旺盛なAI・データ
センター関連需要を背景に、半導体製造装置を含む生産用機械などが増産したものの、熊本地震の影
響を打ち返せなかった。消費財出荷指数の低下については、4～5月の耐久財特需が一巡したことで反
動減の動きが生じた。非耐久財の出荷は前月から増加したものの、7月の落ち込みも踏まえると、 基調
としては力強さを欠いていると言えよう。長雨や低温、といった天候不順により生鮮野菜価格が上昇
しているほか、食肉も値上がりが大きく、こうした手に取る機会が多い品目の価格上昇が家計の節約
志向を強め、個人消費の下押し圧力となっているとみられる。9月についても、食品のほか、原油・石
油化学製品等のコスト高の影響で幅広い品目の価格上昇が加速する見込みであり、個人消費は冴えな
い動きが続く可能性がある。 
以上のように、7～9月期成長率は低い伸びに抑えられる公算が大きい。前述のとおり原油・ナフサ
の輸入増、 企業の在庫調整といった動きはGDP統計上、成長率に対してマイナスに寄与する。加えて、
熊本地震による生産減や天候要因による消費支出抑制の動きが下押し要因になる見込みだ。 
次回の 「みずほGDPナウ」については、11月16日に公表される7～9月期GDP1次速報を挟んで、10～
12月期GDPのリアルタイム予測に取り組む。10月分の鉱工業生産や消費活動指数の結果等を踏まえて
推計し、12月中旬頃のレポート発刊を予定している。原油高等を受けた物価上昇圧力の強まりや堅調
なAI・データセンター関連需要など、プラス・マイナス双方の影響が交錯する中、ナウキャストによ
る景気動向の把握はより重要なものとなるだろう。

［参考文献］

けて～」 、内閣府経済社会総合研究所「経済分析」第208号

～DFMを用いた日本のGDPナウキャスティング～」 、 みずほリサーチ＆テクノロジーズ 『みずほ

時点で 7～9 月期 GDP は前期比＋0.0％と推計～」、みずほリサーチ＆テクノロジーズ

1 ステップワイズ法により使用データを採択した。詳細は太田ほか（2024）を参照されたい。今後のモデルの予測精度のパフォ
ーマンス評価等を踏まえ、採択するデータについては見直しを行う可能性がある。 
2 詳細については、服部直樹「年率＋1.4％と 1 次速報から小幅に上方修正（4～6 月期2 次QE）」（2026 年9 月8 日）を参照され
たい。 
3 太田他（2024）で示した枠組みと同様、図表 2 の折れ線が各時点における実質GDP 成長率の予測値であり、月次の経済指標が
新たに公表されたり改定されたりすることで予測値が更新される。棒グラフは、予測値の改定幅、すなわち前回予測との差を各
月次経済指標で寄与度分解したもので、寄与度を合計するとモデル予測値の改定幅と一致する。なお、 「みずほGDP ナウ」は各
月次経済指標についても公表前に予測値を計算しており、各指標の寄与度はモデル予測値と実績値の乖離から計算されることか
ら、寄与度の符号は経済指標の変化の方向とは必ずしも一致しない。

## Document 153: 4541晟田 台新

### Page 1

本刊載之報告為台新投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬台新投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113) 金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

晟田(4541)：航太及半導體客戶需求延續，帶動 2027 年獲利成長 
建議：中立轉買進

一、晟田為航太精密機械零件加工廠 
晟田成立於 1991 年 8 月，實收資本額 6.76 億元，截至
2Q26 底，員工約 686 位。主要從事航太零組件(包括發
動機機匣與懸塊、起落架)及機械零組件(包括半導體真
空泵浦零組件、食品飲料填充包裝設備零組件、工業自
動化精密傳動零組件等產品)之製造加工與銷售等業務。
2025 年前五大客戶為漢翔(2634)、日本 E 客戶、
SLS(Safran Landing Systems)、SPP (6355.JP)及長榮
航太(2645)。2026 年前 9 個月產品別營收佔比為航太零
組件(客供料) 48%，航太零組件(自購料) 25%，半導體
設備零組件 23%，其他 4%。

二 、 夏季電價及費用認列影響獲利 ， 預估3Q26 稅後 EPS 
1.26 元 
晟田 3Q26 營收 5.6 億元(+1.9%QoQ，+30.9%YoY)，
季增主因為受惠航太客供料客戶拉貨動能成長及半導體
客戶需求提升。獲利方面，儘管產品組合持續優化，惟
受到夏季電價及籌資相關費用認列影響，預估營業利益
1.01 億元(-6%QoQ，+97.1%YoY)。假設匯率持穩，預
估稅後淨利 0.85 億元(-1%QoQ，+34.4%YoY)， 稅後EPS 
1.26 元。 
三、產品組合持續優化，預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.3 元 
展望 4Q26，半導體方面，考量客戶需求持續強勁，有
望帶動出貨動能；航太方面，波音(BA.US) 737 系列機
型月產能有望於 2026 年底自 42 架提升至 47 架，另，
根據空中巴士(EADSY.US)規劃，2026 年預計交付 870
架，截至 Sep-26 交付達成率約 6 成，預期 4Q26 將加
速交機 ， 帶動航太零組件拉貨需求 。 整體而言 ， 預估4Q26
營收 5.62 億元(+0.5%QoQ，+20.7%YoY)。獲利方面，
隨產品組合持續優化，且無電價因素干擾，預估營業利
益 1.05 億元(+3.9%QoQ，+28.6%YoY)。 假設匯率持穩，
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.3 元。 
四、需求延續，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 4.51 元 
展望後市，儘管航太供應鏈仍受原物料短缺、人力及產
能瓶頸影響，惟波音及空巴在手訂單仍維持高位，顯示
終端需求具支撐。隨供應鏈瓶頸逐步緩解，預期將帶動
航太零組件拉貨需求回升，有利晟田營運成長。半導體
方面，受惠終端客戶持續擴大資本支出，帶動相關產品
需求穩定成長。整體而言，預估 2026 年營收 21.2 億元
(+29.1%YoY)。獲利方面，隨產品組合持續優化，預估
毛利率 25.1%，稅後淨利 3.1 億元(+122.9%YoY)，稅後
EPS 4.51 元。 
展望 2027 年，考量航空引擎零組件客戶需求強勁，晟
田規劃擴充相關產能，新廠有望於 2H27 投產；半導體
方面，持續優化相關產能，以因應客戶需求成長。整體
而言，預估 2027 年營收 23.5 億元(+10.7%YoY)。獲利
方面，考量新廠投產後折舊費用增加，預估毛利率年減
至 24.8%， 稅後淨利3.3 億元(+9%YoY)， 稅後EPS 4.58
元(以增資稀釋後股本計算)。 
五、中立轉買進理由 
評價方面，以 2027 年 EPS 估算，目前 PE 約為 13X，
介於晟田歷史區間 12X~20X 下緣 ， 調升至買進評等 ， 目
標價 75 元(2026F PE 17X)。

(3) 預估 2026 年 EPS 2 4.93 元、 2027 年 EPS 42. 45 元 ， 投 資 建 議
買 進 ， 目 標 價 1,6 00 元 。  
3Q26 營收 持 續 成 長 ， 財 務 營 運 槓 桿 與 獲 利 結 構 持 續 優 化：  
2Q26 營收 68. 55 億元 (YoY+90.30%) ，主 要 源 於 美 國 與 日 本 兩 大
高 價 值 市 場 銷 量 顯 著 成 長 ， 團 隊 擴 編 與 精 準 醫 學 行 銷 策 略 效 益
大 舉 顯 現。毛 利 率 94.2 3%，受 惠 美 國 5M2 6 調升藥價 ，以 及 CMS
回 沖， 營 業 費 用 33.00 億元 (YoY +60.68%) ，增 加 主 因 延 續 團 隊
擴 編 、 商 業 推 廣 以 及 依 會 計 準 則 認 列 限 制 員 工 權 利 新 股 約2 億
多 元 ， 增 幅 遠 低 於 營 收 成 長， 展 現 高 營 運 槓 桿 之 強 勁 爆 發 力。  
ET 新 適 應 症 在 全 球 步 入 收 割 期 ， 加 上 PMF 收 案 順 利 與 肝 癌 術
後 100% 防 復 發 數 據 ， 中 長 期 佈 局 完 整：  
原 發 性 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 為 過 去 30 年 來 缺 乏 新 藥 之 領 域，公 司
於 05/12/2026 率 先 取 得 台 灣 ET 藥 證 ； 日 本 MHLW 專 家 會 議 於
07/2026 通 過 審 查 後 ， 已 於 08/24/2026 正 式 獲 日 本 核 准 用 於 所
有成人 ET 患者 ， 包 括 未 曾 接 受 治 療 者 ； 隨 後 美 國 FDA 亦 正 式
核 准 用 於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 ， 成 為 近 30 年首款 ET 新 藥 並 具 7
年 孤 兒 藥 獨 佔 期；中 國 與 韓 國 則 分 別 預 計 於 11/2026 及 12/2026
取 證 ， 並 準 備 於 澳 洲 啟 動 PV 及 ET 雙 適 應 症 藥 證 申 請 。 
早 期 原 發 性 骨 髓 纖 維 化(PMF) 三 期 臨 床 HOPE -PMF 於 06/2026
完 成 收 案 且 超 收 至 160 人 (原 目 標 150 人 )，預計 2027 年 完 成 試
驗、 2028 年 中 申 請 藥 證 。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 15 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
因 應 企 業 永 續 經 營 所 帶 來 的 挑 戰 與 機 會，整 合 各 方 利 害 關 係 人 關 注 之 議 題，
將 展 開 「 永 續 經 營(ESG) 計 畫 」 ， 成 立 企 業 永 續 發 展 委 員 會 ， 聚 焦 於 五 大 策
略 ， 連 結 聯 合 國 「 永 續 發 展 目 標 SDGs 」 ， 以 企 業 核 心 精 神 與 專 業 ， 落 實 永
續 經 營 各 項 目 標，促 進 永 續 的 經 濟 成 長、社 會 發 展 與 環 境 保 護，提 升 企 業 競
爭 力 與 發 揮 影 響 力 。 
公 司 簡 介 ： 
藥華藥主要致力於研發和生產新一代創新型生物藥，特別聚焦於血液腫瘤
領 域 。 公 司 旗 艦 產 品 為 Ropeginterferon alfa -2b ( 簡稱 Ropeg ，商品名
Besremi) ， 是 一 個 創 新 的 長 效 型 干 擾 素 藥 物 ， 目 前 已 在 美 國 、 歐 洲 、 日 本 、
韓 國 、 台 灣 及 中 國 等 全 球 約 50 個 國 家 取 得 藥 證 ， 用 於 治 療 成 人 真 性 紅 血 球
增多症 (Polycythemia Vera, PV) ， 並 已 陸 續 於 台 灣 、 日 本 及 美 國 取 得 原 發 性
血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 藥 證 。  
Ropeg 是 藥 華 藥 自 行 研 發、並 擁 有 全 球 獨 家 權 利 的 新 藥。公 司 利 用 其 特 有 的
長 效 型 蛋 白 質 藥 物 技 術 平 台 ， 成 功 開 發 出Ropeg 。 過 去 PV 的 治 療 藥 物 有 限
且 常 伴 隨 副 作 用 ， 包 含 老 藥 HU 及 尚 未 獲 PV 適 應 症 批 准 的 干 擾 素 藥 物 ， 與
美 國 藥 廠 Incyte 開 發 的 Jakafi (ruxolitinib) 。 2019 年， Ropeg 獲 得 歐 洲 藥 品
管理局 (EMA) 核 准 上 市 ， 並 在 2021 年 11 月 獲 得 美 國 FDA 的 核 准 ， 成 為 第
一個被 FDA 批 准 用 於 所 有 PV 成 人 的 治 療 藥 物 ， 不 受 之 前 治 療 史 的 限 制 ，
也 是 第 一 個 被 FDA 核 准 特 別 用 於 PV 的 干 擾 素 療 法 ， 且 已 連 續 四 年 獲NCCN
治 療 指 引 推 薦 。 
相 較 於 傳 統 干 擾 素 ，Ropeg 具 有 更 長 的 半 衰 期 ， 因 此 可 以 延 長 給 藥 間 隔 ， 提
高 病 患 的 便 利 性 及 順 從 性，同 時 也 展 現 出 更 優 異 的 療 效。 在 7.3 年 的 臨 床 追
蹤 數 據 顯 示 76% 的 完 全 血 液 學 反 應 率(CHR) ， 並 能 持 續 降 低 JAK2 基 因 突 變
負 擔 。 藥 華 藥 在 美 國 、 歐 洲 、 日 本 及 台 灣 等 地 設 有 子 公 司 ， 負 責 藥 物 的 銷
售 與 推 廣 。Ropeg 目 前 是 公 司 最 主 要 的 營 收 來 源 ， 佔 比 超 過9 成 。 此 外 也 積
極拓展 Ropeg 的 其 他 適 應 症 ， 包 含 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 及 骨 髓 纖 維 化(MF) 等。。 
3Q26 營 收 季 增，9 月 未 含 CMS 調 整 仍 創 新 高，財 務 營 運 槓 桿 與 獲 利 結 構 持
續 優 化 ：  
2Q2 6 營收 68. 55 億元 (YoY+90.30%) ， 主 要 源 於 美 國 與 日 本 兩 大 高 價 值 市 場
銷 量 顯 著 成 長 ， 團 隊 擴 編 與 精 準 醫 學 行 銷 策 略 效 益 大 舉 顯 現 。 毛 利 率
94.2 3%， 受 惠 美 國 5M2 6 調 升 藥 價，以及 CMS 回沖， 營 業 費 用 33.00 億元
(YoY +60.68%) ， 增 加 主 因 延 續 團 隊 擴 編 、 商 業 推 廣 以 及 依 會 計 準 則 認 列 限
制 員 工 權 利 新 股 約 2 億多元 ，增 幅 遠 低 於 營 收 成 長，展 現 高 營 運 槓 桿 之 強 勁
爆發力 。  
ET 新 適 應 症 在 全 球 步 入 收 割 期 ， 併 購 予 宇 切 入 新 興 療 法 ， 加 上PMF 收案
順 利 與 肝 癌 術 後 100% 防 復 發 數 據 ， 中 長 期 佈 局 完 整：  
原 發 性 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 為過去 30 年 來 缺 乏 新 藥 之 領 域 ， 公 司 於05/12/2026
率 先 取 得 台 灣 ET 藥 證 ， 用 於 HU 抗 藥 性 或 無 法 耐 受 之 成 人 ET 病 人 ； 日 本
MHLW 專 家 會 議 於 07/2026 通 過 審 查 ， 破 例 同 意 將 全 球 三 期 SURPASS -ET
與 北 美 單 臂 二 期 XC 數 據 合 併 評 估 ， 並 已 於 08/24/2026 正 式 獲 日 本 核 准 用

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 15 日

3 群益投顧 
於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 ， 包 括 未 曾 接 受 治 療 者 ； 隨 後 美 國FDA 亦 正 式 核 准 Ropeg
用 於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 ， 成 為 近 30 年首款 ET 新 藥 ， 並 具 7 年 孤 兒 藥 獨 佔
期，配 合 芝 加 哥 誓 師 大 會 已 完 成 之 團 隊 培 訓 與 推 廣 文 宣，取 證 後 已 立 即 展 開
商 業 推 廣 ； 其 他 市 場 方 面 ， 中 國 大 陸 與 韓 國ET 審 查 同 步 進 行 ， 分 別 預 計 於
11/2026 及 12/2026 取 證 ， 同 時 準 備 於 澳 洲 啟 動 PV 及 ET 雙 適 應 症 藥 證 申
請。早 期 原 發 性 骨 髓 纖 維 化(PMF) 三 期 臨 床 HOPE -PMF 於 06/2026 完 成 收 案
且 超 收 至 160 人 (原目標 150 人 )， 預 計 2027 年 完 成 試 驗 、2028 年 中 申 請 藥
證。  
在 實 體 腫 瘤 領 域 ，B 型 肝 炎 相 關 肝 癌(HCC) 術 後 防 復 發 一 期 試 驗 ，Ropeg 序
列合併 Anti -PD1 在 15 名 患 者 中 位 追 蹤 1,024 天 ， 創 下 100% 無 復 發 存 活 率
(RFS) 之 重 大 突 破 。PEG -IL -2 (P11838) 、 PD -1-IL -2v 、 ADC 及 KRAS TCR -T
將於 2026 -2027 年 陸 續 申 請 IND 。  
藥 華 藥 近 期 斥 資 約 1.68 億 元 收 購 予 宇 生 技 100% 股 權 ， 取 得 脂 質 奈 米 顆 粒
(LNP) 藥 物 遞 送 平 台 、 研 發 設 備 及 專 業 團 隊 ， 並 納 入 兩 項 新 藥 研 發 專 案 。 予
宇成立於 2019 年， LNP 平 台 涵 蓋 配 方 設 計 、 微 流 體 製 程 與 分 析 方 法 ， 可 支
援 mRNA 、 saRNA 及 circRNA 等 多 元 RNA 藥 物 開 發 ； 原 有 FR -202 與 FR -
201 兩 項 專 案 分 別 鎖 定 端 粒 生 物 學 疾 病 與 威 爾 森 氏 症 ， 預 計 於 2027 年及
2028 年 提 出 IND 。  
此 項 收 購 對 公 司 營 運 具 三 大 正 面 意 義 ：(1) 收 購 金 額 僅 約 1.68 億 元 ， 相 對 公
司單季逾 25 億 元 淨 利 規 模 ， 財 務 負 擔 極 低 ；(2) 取 得 遞 送 系 統 自 主 能 力 ， 可
與公司既有蛋白質藥物製造資源整合，從研發早期即同步規劃藥物設計與
後 續 製 造 ；(3) 延 伸 研 發 版 圖 至 In Vivo CAR -T 此 一 國 際 大 廠 積 極 布 局 的 領
域 ， 提 升 中 長 期 選 擇 權 價 值 ， 亦 為 後 續 研 發 產 品 線 布 局 增 加 深 度 與 廣 度 。 
注 射 筆 上 市 與 潛 力 患 者 數 上 修 雙 輪 驅 動，配 合 真 實 世 界 數 據 防止 惡 化，鞏 固
Ropeg 在 PV 領 域 之 地 位 ：  
Ropeg 在 PV 適 應 症 已 於 全 球 約 50 個 國 家 獲 批 ， 加 拿 大 亦 於 07/02/2026 
順 利 取 證 。 美 國 FDA 於 06/2026 核准 Ropeg 注 射 筆 ， 並 於 07/2026 正 式 出
貨 開 賣，注 射 筆 具 備 極 細 針 頭、精 準 調 劑 及 一 次 性 自 動 縮 回 防 針 扎 設 計，短
期 可 加 速 替 換 傳 統 預 充 充 填 針 筒 Prefilled Syringe ， 中 長 期 將 提 升 患 者 長 期
用 藥 依 從 性 與 留 存 率 。 
武田製藥 (Takeda) 競品 Rusfertide (擬 似 鐵 調 素)預 計 於 08/2026 獲 批，藥 華 藥
指出  Rusfertide 僅 定 位 於 配 合 放 血 之 被 動 症 狀 控 制 ， 而 Ropeg 為 一 線 首 選
積 極 療 法 ， 真 實 世 界 數 據 證 實 Ropeg 合 併 治 療 可 使 患 者 疾 病 惡 化成 骨 髓 纖
維化  (MF )的 概 率 維 持 在 0%。最 新 流 行 病 學 文 獻 將 美 國 MPN 總 潛 力 患 者 數
上修至 34.70 萬人 ( 原 30.50 萬人 )， 競 品 進 入市 場 將 大 幅 提 升 早 期 治 療 之 病
識 感 與 診 斷 率 ， 反 而 有 助 於 Ropeg 進 一 步 擴 大 一 線 市 場 壟 斷 地 位 。 
藥 華 藥 與 AOP 仲 裁 案 ： 
關於 AOP 第 二 階 段 仲 裁 判 斷 ， 藥 華 藥 認 為 其 程 序 存 在 違 反 程 序 正 義 及 聽 審
權 之 重 大 瑕 疵 ， 目 前 正 與 外 部 律 師 團 隊 嚴 謹 評 估 ， 將 在 法 定 時 限 內 向 德 國
法 蘭 克 福 高 等 法 院 提 起 「 撤 銷 第 二 階 段 仲 裁 判 斷 之 訴 」 。 
由 於 法 律 程 序 仍 在 評 估 中 ， 現 階 段 無 法 判 斷 或 預 測 該 裁 定 金 額 發 生 的 時 間
點 ， 後 續 發 展 視 訴 訟 進 度 而 定 。 公 司 與AOP 之 合 約 目 前 仍 處 於 有 效 狀 態 ，

4 群益投顧 
對 於 任 何 有 助 於 妥 善 解 決 爭 議 、 維 護 股 東 權 益 之 方 案 均 持 開 放 態 度 。 歐 洲
仍 為 藥 華 藥 極 為 重 視 之 核 心 市 場 ， 將 綜 合 評 估 法 規 要 求 、 商 業 推 廣 與 契 約
關 係 ， 持 續 規 劃 並 評 估 於 歐 洲 申 請 ET 藥 證 之 可 行 性 。 
AOP 在 8/7/2026 發 布 的 官 方 新 聞 稿 表 示 ， 藥 華 藥 延 遲 造 成 的 損 害 約€82M ，
2019 -2022 過 高 價 格 所 產 生 的 退 款 約 €31M ， AOP 原 本 應 支 付 給 藥 華 藥 的
profit sharing 約 17M 可 用 來 抵 銷 ； 最 終 AOP 表 示 自 己 不 用 付 款 給 藥 華 藥 ，
並 認 為 藥 華 藥 目 前 對 AOP 的 責 任 已 進 入 「 三 位 數 百 萬 歐 元 」 範 圍 。 推 論 藥
華 藥 實 際 曝 險 金 額 Net Set -off 後 約 為 €97.48M (約新台幣 36 億元 )。  
AOP 提 出 延 伸 主 張 ， 若 將 仲 裁 庭 對 2019 -2022 年 價 格 爭 議( 最高 900% 超額
定 價 之 認 定)的 計 算 邏 輯 套 用 至 2023 -2025 年供貨， AOP 主 張 藥 華 藥 需 再 退
還約 €65M ， 此 項 目 僅 為 AOP 單 方 面 之 延 伸 主 張 ， 非 現 階 段 確 定 之 仲 裁 判
決。  
藥 華 藥 已 從 純 研 發 型 生 技 公 司 轉 型 為 具 備 強 勁 商 業 化 現 金 流 之 藥 廠 ， 一 次
性認列約 36 億 元 損 失 衝 擊 ， 不 至 於 構 成 營 運 生 存 危 機。

投 資 建 議：(1)07/2026 於 美 國 開 賣 Ropeg 注 射 筆 提 升 患 者 用 藥 順 應 性 與 留 存
率 ， 推 升 低 風 險 PV/ET 患 者 滲 透 率 ， 為 2H26 營 收 加 速 引 擎 ；(2)ET 新 適 應
症 迎 來 多 國 藥 證 收 割，開 啟 第 二 成 長 曲 線，台 灣、日本 取 證 後，美國 FDA 亦
核 准 用 於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 並 具 7 年 孤 兒 藥 獨 佔 期，中 國 與 韓 國 分 別 預 計 於
11/2026 與 12/2026 取 證 ， 並 準 備 於 澳 洲 啟 動 PV 及 ET 雙 適 應 症 藥 證 申 請。
(3) 預估 2026 年 EPS 2 4.93 元、 2027 年 EPS 42. 45 元 ， 投 資 建 議 買 進 ， 目 標
價 1,6 00 元 。

個股報告

7 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15 %

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

增 34.9%，營益率上修 0.5ppt至 11.9%，營業利益上修 4.4%至 230.55億
元，季減 28.1%，年增 338.4%，稅後 EPS 上修 4.2%至 2.04 元，季減
26.1%，年增 280.3%，4Q26營益率季減係反映汽油消費轉淡，煉油利差收
斂，惟沙烏地 OSP 折價擴大至 5 美元，汽油價差優於預期，因而上修獲利。 
預估 1Q27 稅後 EPS 上修 3.5%至 1.78 元 
展望 1Q27 農曆年假工作天數較少，不過受損的煉油產能難以恢復，冬天取
暖用油需求仍強，柴油供需持續偏緊，維繫價差高檔，預估 1Q27 營收維持
1833.16億元，季減 5.5%，年增 13.2%，營益率上修 0.3ppt至 10.7%，營
業利益上修 2.9%至 196.66億元，季減 14.7%，年減 19.8%，稅後 EPS上
修 3.5%至 1.78元，季減 12.9%，年減 17.0%。獲利年減，乃因美伊戰爭爆
發導致 1Q26基期較高，不過 1Q27獲利仍遠優於 2025年各季表現。 
煉油產能受損難以恢復，供應吃緊，利差展望佳 
煉油產能受損難以恢復，供給吃緊，估 2026 年營收上修 1.1%至 7826.12
億元，年增 25%，稅後 EPS上修 1.2%至 9.12元，年增 780.1%，推估配
息 6.39元，殖利率達 5.2%以上，仍具吸引力。預期 2027年營收 7559.99
億元，年減 3.4%，加計南亞科現金股利挹注，稅後 EPS上修 10.9%至 6.84
元，雖年減 25.1%，遠優於 2025年獲利低迷狀態。目前本淨比 2.17倍，處
於歷史本淨比 1.0倍-3.0倍區間中間，重申買進建議，目標價由 125元上調
至 160元，本淨比由 2.2倍上修至 2.8倍及 2027年預估每股淨值 56.36元
推得，本淨比上修係反映煉油利差亮麗，提振獲利，評價上修。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 稅後 EPS 2.76 元，年增 206.3%，創單季新高。而煉油利差仍
佳，預估 4Q26/2026 稅後 EPS 上修 4.2%/1.2%至 2.04 元/9.12 元。 
⧫  
柴油價差維持高檔，且加計南亞科股利收入挹注， 預估 1Q27/2027 年
稅後 EPS 各上修 3.5%/10.9%至 1.78 元/6.84 元。 
⧫  
煉油產能受損 ，成品油供應吃緊， 提振利差，重申買進，目標價 上調
至 160 元，基於 2.8 倍本淨比及 2027 年估每股淨值 56.36 元推得。

營運分析
煉油利差亮麗，台塑化 3Q26 稅後 EPS 續增至 2.76 元，季增 26.5%，年增
206.3%，優於預期 1.1%
台塑化(6505)公布 3Q26自結獲利續揚，動能亮麗。3Q26營收 2436.54億元(原估 2351.33億元)，
季增 33.2%，年增 49%，優於預期 3.6%，儘管成品油均價季跌 4.7%至 128.9美元，小幅回檔，
不過原油供應恢復，開工率季增 24.7ppt至 91.2%，成品油銷售量季增 43.7%至 4832.3萬桶，帶
動煉油營收季增 35%，至於烯烴事業營收季增 17%，乃係原料供應增加，開工率季增 6.5ppt 至
39.6%，銷售量季增 26.1%至 78.3 萬噸，至於公用事業營收季增 12.3%，反映夏季電費較高，及
售電量與售氣量均增溫。
3Q26營益率季增 0.7ppt至 13.17%(原估 13.19%)，符合預期，營業利益 320.82億元(原估 310.15

億元)，季增 40.2%，年增 245.6%，優於預期 3.4%，業外現金股利收入約 6.4億元，稅後 EPS為
2.76元(原估 2.73元)，季增 26.5%，年增 206.3%，優於預期 1.1%，獲利創單季新高。3Q26營益
率回升係因美伊談判陷入膠著，中東煉油產能恢復延後，而俄羅斯煉油廠遭受烏克蘭攻擊，產出下
滑，煉油利差自 6 月低點逐步走高，加上沙烏地阿拉伯調降對亞洲原油的官方售價(OSP)，成本下
降，提振 3Q26煉油利差擴大，加上認列 27.9億元存貨評價利益，帶動整體獲利走高。

汽油消費轉淡且歲修干擾，惟整體煉油利差仍優於預期，預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 上
修 4.2%至 2.04 元，年增 280.3% 
展望 4Q26 美伊談判陷入膠著，難以擺脫僵局狀態，且沙烏地與葉門胡塞組織戰火擴大，預期中東
局勢仍將處於不穩定狀態，油價延續高檔整理，而原油供應應可處於穩定狀態，且歐洲積極建立柴
油庫存，以滿足冬季取暖需求，有利柴油價格強勢。另一方面，儘管汽油消費進入淡季，不過煉油
廠歲修較為密集，開工率降低，且全球汽油庫存偏低，及中國限制成品油出口，帶動汽油價格 於
10 月回彈，汽油價差優於預期，而煉油基礎單元於 10 月至 11 月進行歲修，煉油開工率由 3Q26
的 91%降至 75%，產銷量下滑，至於烯烴事業營運平淡，預估開工率維持在 35-40%左右。維持
4Q26營收 1939.85億元之預估，季減 20.4%，年增 34.9%，營益率上修 0.5ppt至 11.9%%(原估
11.4%)，營業利益上修 4.4%至 230.55億元(原估 220.85億元)，季減 28.1%，年增 338.4%，稅後
EPS上修 4.2%至 2.04元(原估 1.96元)，季減 26.1%，年增 280.3%，4Q26營益率季減係反映歲
修干擾，稼動率下滑之影響，惟沙烏地阿拉伯對亞洲原油的官方售價(OSP)折價幅度擴大至 5 美元，
成本下降，整體獲利仍優於原先預期，台塑化 4Q26獲利表現，將遠優於 4Q25同期。

預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 上修 1.2%至 9.12 元，年增 780.1% 
展望 2026 年，美伊戰爭引發的中東局勢動盪可能往常態化發展，中東產能運作受衝擊，且俄羅斯
煉油廠遭受烏克蘭攻擊，產出下滑，而歐美老舊煉油廠相繼關廠退出市場，中國亦有淘汰老舊煉油
廠產能規劃，供給受限，而美國恐限制柴油出口，成品油供給吃緊，而成品油需求穩健，維繫煉油
利差。台塑化獲利回溫，預估 2026 年營收上修 1.1%至 7826.12 億元(原估 7740.90 億元)，年增
25%，營益率上修 0.1ppt 至 13.1%(原估 13.0%)，營業利益上修 2.0%至 1025.35 億元(原估
1004.97億元)，年增 842.5%，稅後 EPS上修 1.2%至 9.12元(原估 9.01元)，年增 780.1%，年底
淨值 55.91元(原估 55.80元)。

展望 1Q27 農曆年假工作天數較少，成品油產出下滑，不過美伊和談進展不順，中東政局動盪，預
期油價延續高檔格局，中東及俄羅斯煉油產能受損情況則難以快速恢復，柴油供給受限，至於需求
端，冬天取暖用油需求仍強，預期柴油供需仍將延續緊俏短缺格局。有利柴油價格及價差延續強勢，

爭爆發，推動 2026 年 3 月之後成品油價格飆漲，價差擴大，墊高基期所致，不過獲利仍遠優於
2025年各季表現。

預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 上修 10.9%至 6.84 元，年減 25.1% 
展望 2027 年，OPEC+增產原油恐壓抑油價，不過中東局勢動盪常態化，加上 OECD 國家回補原
油庫存，將支撐油價，預期油價延續偏高檔整理，惟預期中東及俄羅斯受損的煉油產能可能逐步恢
復運作，煉油利差將較 2026 年滑落，但仍會優於美伊戰爭前的水準，預估 2027 年營收維持
7559.99億元，年減 3.4%，營益率上修 0.1ppt至 9.1%(原估 9.0%)，年減 4.0ppt，營業利益上修
0.8%至 684.10 億元(原估 678.60 億元)，年減 33.3%，業外方面，加計持有南亞(1303)及南亞科
(2408)預期將配發之現金股利合計推估約 72.7億元之挹注，稅後 EPS上修 10.9%至 6.84元(原估
6.17元)，年減 25.1%，年底淨值 56.36元(原估 55.66元)。 
若以 70%股利配發率計算，推估 2027年台塑化將配息 6.39元，以目前股價計算，殖利率達 5.2%
以上，仍具吸引力。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：塑化 2026年 10月 19日 FPCC 第5 頁，共 18頁  
中東煉油產能受損及俄羅斯煉油廠受攻擊，維繫煉油價差表現，提振台塑化獲利 
煉油事業營運方面，2026 年 2 月底美伊戰爭爆發，驅動油價大漲，成品油價格也隨之走高，其中
柴油報價自 2月底約 90美元一路走高至 4月上旬的 220美元以上，柴油價差也自 14美元擴大至
140美元，汽油報價則自 2月底約 77美元一路走高至 3月下旬 160美元以上，汽油價差自 1美元
擴大至 48 美元，隨著美伊於 4月啟動和談，油價處於高檔震盪，成品油價格也同樣處於高位，柴
油價差則自 4月初高點收斂至 50-60美元區間，汽油價差則收斂至 20-30美元區間，2026/6/12美
伊雙方針對停火條件達成共識，並於 6/17正式簽署包含 14個要點諒解備忘錄 MOU，油價及成品
油價格見到進一步修正，柴油價差收斂至 30-35美元區間，汽油價差收斂至 15-20美元。 
7月以來美伊戰爭再起，油價大漲，成品價格隨之走高，價差再度擴大，柴油價差擴大至 50-60美
元，汽油價差擴大至 25-30美元，8月及 9月柴油價差擴大至 60-70美元，10月持續維持在 60美
元以上，汽油價差於 8月及 9月維持在 20-25美元之間，10月則突破 30美元以上，優於預期，汽
油價差擴大乃因全球汽油產能歲修較為密集，開工率降低，且全球汽油庫存偏低，以及中國限制成
品油出口，帶動汽油價格回彈。儘管柴油及汽油價差自 4月初的高點收斂，不過對比美伊戰前，柴
油價差多處於 15-25美元區間，汽油價差多處於 10-15美元，近期柴油及汽油價差仍高於美伊戰前
水準。另一方面，基於 OPEC+決議原油增產，市場競爭加劇，沙烏地阿拉伯則調降對亞洲原油的
官方售價(OSP)，6月為溢價 15.5美元，7月為溢價 9.5美元，8月降至折價 1.5美元，9月折價
2.0美元，10月延續折價 2.0美元，11月折價幅度再擴大至 5.0美元，推估 12月仍將維持折價狀
態，亞洲國家原油採購成本降低，可維繫煉油利差處於高檔水準。展望後市，由於中東煉油設備受
損，成品油產能仍須一段時間才有機會逐漸恢復，且俄羅斯煉油廠遭受烏克蘭攻擊，產出下滑，成
品油供給相對偏緊，美國川普總統施壓歐洲國家釋出柴油庫存，顯示供需缺口擴大，預期柴油及汽
油價差可維持高於美伊戰前水準，有利台塑化煉油事業獲利維持亮麗表現，較 2025年大幅改善。

南亞科(2408)股價波動劇烈，先保守以 2Q26 底收盤價作為評價基礎，推估台塑化
2026 年底淨值 55.91 元 
台塑化藉由”透過其他綜合損益按公允價值衡量之金融資產 -非流動”會計科目持有南亞科(2408)股權
約 10.81%，每季底依據南亞科股價變動造成的持股市值變化將反映在台塑化之股權權益及淨值。
台塑化分別於 2025/10/28、11/11及 12/31公告相繼已處分 2.5萬、1.8萬及約 1.9萬張南亞科持
股，平均處分價格各為 98.67元、145.09元及 169.46元，台塑化目前持有南亞科股權降至 8.81%。
簡單試算，若南亞科股價每變動 10元，將造成台塑化淨值變動約 0.29元。 
若以南亞科最新 (2026/10/17) 股價 447.5元計算，對比 2026/6/30收盤價 452.5元，台塑化持股
8.81%的評價利益將較 2Q26底減少約 13.64億元，將導致台塑化淨值減少約 0.14元。由於南亞科
股價波動較為劇烈，先保守以 2026/6/30 收盤價作為評價基礎，推估台塑化 2026 年底淨值為
55.91元(原估 55.80元) ，2026年淨值大幅提升乃係反映持股南亞科股價大漲後的評價利益貢獻。

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：塑化 2026年 10月 19日 FPCC 第7 頁，共 18頁  
產業概況  
美伊和談進展反覆，油價於 6 月大幅下挫超過 30%後，3Q26 大幅反彈 
美國與伊朗於 2026年 4月初開始啟動停火協議談判，經歷過 2個多月斡旋及拉鋸，於 2026/6/12
雙方針對停火條件達成共識，並於 6/17正式簽署包含 14個要點諒解備忘錄 MOU，承諾在 60天內
達成最終全面協議。在美伊進行停火談判期間，荷姆茲海峽仍處於封鎖狀態，全球原油供應仍有短
缺，不過 OECD 主要國家以原油庫存支應需求，加上美伊和談的預期心理，油價即面臨震盪拉回
的修正壓力，期間美伊談判反覆幾度再推動油價反彈，但都未能突破 3月高點，呈現高檔整理格局，
美伊雙方談判一度進展順利，油價於 5月初高點 100美元以上，一路修正至 6月下旬波段低點，西
德州原油跌破 70 美元，布蘭特原油跌破 75 美元，波段跌幅均超過 30%，走勢疲弱，已跌至
2026/2/28 美伊戰爭爆發前的油價區間 65-70 美元。3Q26 以來，美伊談判陷入反覆，並再次交火，
油價再反彈回升至 100美元以上。近期美伊戰火停息，美國計畫改採對伊朗經濟與貿易制裁手段，
美伊雙方對和談條件的歧見仍深，短期難以達成和談共識，談判陷入膠著，油價將呈現高檔震盪格
局。 
圖 6：西德州與布蘭特原油價格走勢圖(美元/桶)

資料來源：Platts、元大投顧整理

美國釋出戰備庫存壓制油價，亦可望於低檔回補庫存，預期油價上檔壓力區 100-
110 美元，下檔支撐區 70-75 美元 
另一方面，觀察 OECD 國家原油庫存趨勢，美伊戰爭開打後，各國政府為因應荷姆茲海峽封鎖造
成的原油供應短缺，先提供原油庫存滿足原油需求，造成原油庫存於近 4 個月庫存大減 2.49 億桶
至 25.59億桶，為 2024年 2月以來新低，也低於過去 4年平均的 27.7億桶。3Q26油價一度大漲，
為壓制油價漲勢，美國能源部宣布將從戰略石油儲備釋出 4000 萬桶原油，並敦促歐洲成員國釋出
戰備原油儲備，預期 OECD 國家原油庫存恐延續滑落格局。荷姆茲海峽仍有油輪通行，加上替代
油路，中東產油國原油運輸恢復，供應趨於穩定，亦有機會壓制原油價格漲勢，預期油價 100-110
美元區間將是上檔壓力區。至於油價的支撐區研判落在 70-75美元，乃考量各國政府有動機於油價
相對低檔之際，進行原油庫存回補，可為油價形成支撐。

圖 7：西德州與布蘭特原油價格走勢圖(美元/桶)

資料來源：Platts、元大投顧整理

全球煉油產能受損比率約 6.7%，成品油供應受衝擊 
全球煉油產能約 1.03億桶，主要煉油產能集中在亞太地區占比 38.3%最大，其次為北美 20.9%，
歐洲 11.8%，至於中東煉油產能僅約 1120萬桶，占比約 10.9%為第四大煉油地區，美伊戰爭爆發
後，推估中東受損的煉油產能約 540 萬桶，占比約 5.2%。另一方面，烏俄戰爭演變為長期對抗，
烏克蘭無人機精準打擊俄羅斯煉油設備，俄羅斯煉油總產能約 550 萬桶，推估受損產能約 150 萬
桶，占比約 1.46%。若合計中東及俄羅斯受損的煉油產能，合計約 690萬桶，占比約 6.7%，全球
成品油供應受衝擊，也推動柴油及汽油價格與利差維持高檔。

圖 8：全球煉油產能分布圖

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：塑化 2026年 10月 19日 FPCC 第9 頁，共 18頁  
亞洲廠商缺料減產的乙烯產能損失缺口約 16-17%，供需過剩格局趨於改善 
荷姆茲海峽封鎖期間，原油運輸中斷，高度依賴中東原料供應的亞洲煉油及石化廠商面臨缺料狀況，
包括日本、韓國、台灣、中國及東南亞等均有石化廠商相繼宣布不可抗力因素而減產，調降開工率。
以乙烯產能為例，亞洲乙烯產能佔全球比重約 45%，若因缺料而將開工率由原本正常的 75-80%水
準調降至 50%左右，推估一個月乙烯產能損失造成的乙烯供需缺口約 12.5%，加計中東約 15%乙
烯產能受損而運作不順(假設開工率僅 20%)，就算美洲(包括美國及南美)的石化廠商會伺機拉高開
工率填補，推估全球乙烯一個月實際的缺口約 16-17%。至於其他基礎石化原料因缺料及運作不順
的產能損失大概落在 14-17%不等。整體而言，缺料造成減產的供給限縮，有助於加速庫存去化，
塑化產品供需過剩格局趨於改善。

圖 9：全球石化基礎原料因缺料減產的產能損失推估比率彙整表

資料來源：元大投顧整理

中東受損的石化產能需要時間修復，有利石化產品價格及利差回穩 
美伊停火談判導致近 2026年 6月油價回落重挫，而下游產品買氣縮手，市場陷入觀望，拖累塑化
產品報價同步修正。美伊和談反覆，戰火再起，油價止跌回穩，不過荷姆茲海峽仍有油輪通行，加
上替代油路，原油供應逐漸恢復，亞洲石化廠商缺料緩解，開工率可望逐步提升，產出增加，另外
油價回升，有助於強化塑化產品報價的漲價預期，可止跌回穩。中長期而言，美伊戰爭引發的中東
局勢動盪可能往常態化發展，佔全球石化產能比重約 15%的中東地區，因戰爭受損運作停擺之產
能需要較長時間的修復，實質衝擊影響將擴大，預期供給端擴張幅度可控，有利塑化產品報價及利
差回穩，高於 2026年初美伊戰前水準，看法維持正向。

### Page 10

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：塑化 2026年 10月 19日 FPCC 第10 頁，共 18頁
獲利調整與股票評價
煉油產能受損難以恢復，供應吃緊，利差展望佳，獲利上修
展望 2026 年，美伊戰爭引發的中東局勢動盪可能往常態化發展，中東產能運作受衝擊，且俄羅斯
煉油廠遭受烏克蘭攻擊，產出下滑，而歐美老舊煉油廠相繼關廠退出市場，中國亦有淘汰老舊煉油
廠產能規劃，供給受限，而美國恐限制柴油出口，成品油供給吃緊，而成品油需求穩健，維繫煉油
利差。台塑化獲利回溫，預估 2026 年營收上修 1.1%至 7826.12 億元(原估 7740.90 億元)，年增
25%，營益率上修 0.1ppt 至 13.1%(原估 13.0%)，營業利益上修 2.0%至 1025.35 億元(原估
1004.97億元)，年增 842.5%，稅後 EPS上修 1.2%至 9.12元(原估 9.01元)，年增 780.1%，年底
淨值 55.91元(原估 55.80元)。
展望 2027 年，OPEC+增產原油恐壓抑油價，不過中東局勢動盪常態化，加上 OECD 國家回補原

油庫存，將支撐油價，預期油價延續偏高檔整理，惟預期中東及俄羅斯受損的煉油產能可能逐步恢
復運作，煉油利差將較 2026 年滑落，但仍會優於美伊戰爭前的水準，預估 2027 年營收維持
7559.99億元，年減 3.4%，營益率上修 0.1ppt至 9.1%(原估 9.0%)，年減 4.0ppt，營業利益上修
0.8%至 684.10 億元(原估 678.60 億元)，年減 33.3%，業外方面，加計持有南亞(1303)及南亞科
(2408)預期將配發之現金股利合計推估約 72.7億元之挹注，稅後 EPS上修 10.9%至 6.84元(原估
6.17元)，年減 25.1%，年底淨值 56.36元(原估 55.66元)。若以 70%股利配發率計算，推估 2027
年台塑化將配息 6.39元，以目前股價計算，殖利率達 5.2%以上，仍具吸引力。
對台塑化看法正向，建議買進，目標價由 125元上調至 160元。考量：1)成品油供給吃緊，提振煉
油利差，3Q26營業利益 320.82億元，年增 245.6%，稅後 EPS為 2.76元，年增 206.3%，獲利

創單季新高。2) 沙烏地與葉門胡塞組織戰火擴大，油價高檔，而冬季取暖的柴油需求持續強勁，汽
油庫存偏低，中國限制出口，推動汽油價差擴大，4Q26營業利益上修 4.4%至 230.55億元，年增
338.4%，稅後 EPS 上修 4.2%至 2.04 元，年增 280.3%。預估 2026 年營業利益上修 2.0%至
1025.35億元，年增 842.5%，稅後 EPS上修 1.2%至 9.12元，年增 780.1%，擺脫 2025年獲利低
迷狀態。3)推估 2027年台塑化將配息 6.39元，以目前股價計算，殖利率達 5.2%以上，仍具吸引
力。4)受惠轉投資南亞科及南亞現金股利之貢獻，2027年稅後 EPS上修 10.9%至 6.84元。推估
台塑化 2027年底淨值增至 56.36元，目前本淨比 2.17倍，處於歷史本淨比 1.0倍-3.0倍的區間中
間，重申買進建議，目標價由 125元上調至 160元，本淨比由 2.2倍上修至 2.8倍及 2027年預估
每股淨值 56.36元推得，本淨比上修係反映煉油利差亮麗，提振獲利，評價上修。

公司簡介  
台塑石化股份有限公司(6505.TW)成立於 1992 年，由台塑、南亞、台化等公司共同出資成立，為國內唯一之民營石油煉製
業者，且石油製品及石化原料產能規模位居國內第一。另有合格汽電共生系統，供應台塑企業麥寮廠區各工廠所需之蒸汽、
電力等各項公用流體。公司主要業務分為油品及煉油、烯烴事業。油品銷售方面，主要以國內市場需求為優先， 2025 年銷
貨佔比為台灣 37%、歐洲 14%、新加坡 10%、英國 6%和其他 33%，汽柴油在國內市占率約 22%。塑化事業方面，乙烯
在國內市占率約 64%。主要客戶為台塑、台化、南亞、國喬、台橡、台達化等國內下游石化廠。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 台塑化整體的 ESG風險評級屬於嚴重風險，於 Sustainalytics資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於落後的
位置，亦於石油天然氣煉製行銷行業的公司中排名落後同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 台塑化的整體曝險較高，且略高於該石油天然氣煉製行銷行業的平均水準。在 ESG（環境、社
會與治理）方面，值得關注的實質議題包括：產品與服務、排放、廢水與廢棄物，以及職業健康與安全。  
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：台塑化在針對重大 ESG議題的管理水準和執行力為平均水準。該公司展現出堅定的
ESG 承諾，制定了完善的環境政策，並發布了符合國際永續發展標準的、高品質的 ESG 報告。公司透過董事會或高階
主管層面的委員會（或指定成員）強化了對 ESG 事務的監督。然而，其舉報人機制較為薄弱，顯示出該領域亟需改進。

分析師聲明 
主要負責撰寫本研究報告全文或部分內容之分析師，茲針對本報告所載證券或證券發行機構，於此聲明： (1) 文中所述觀點皆準確反映其個人對各證券或
證券發行機構之看法；(2) 研究部分析師於本研究報告中所提出之特定投資建議或觀點，與其過去、現在、未來薪酬的任何部份皆無直接或間接關聯。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

而得。

元大研究報告於美國僅發送予美國主要投資法人（依據 1934 年《證券交易法》15a-6 號規則及其修正條文與美國證券交易委員會詮釋定義）。美國投資
人若欲進行與本報告所載證券相關之交易，皆必須透過依照 1934年《證券交易法》第 15條及其修正條文登記註冊之券商為之。元大研究報告在台灣由元
大證券投資顧問股份有限公司發佈，在香港則由元大證券 (香港)有限公司發佈。元大證券(香港)係獲香港證券及期貨事務監察委員會核准註冊之券商，並
獲許從事受規管活動，包括第 4 類規管活動（就證券提供意見）。非經元大證券(香港)有限公司書面明示同意，本研究報告全文或部份，不得以任何形式
或方式轉載、轉寄或揭露。

億豐(8464) 
買進, TP 450 (潛在報酬 25.52%)  
買進 
Price(10/20):358.5 
Target Price：450

隨客製化需求持續成長、柬埔寨廠人力恢復至5,000人並開始協助
客戶回補庫存，帶動3Q26營收創下歷史新高，且受惠於營收規模
擴大、產能利用率提升及關稅返還挹注，淨利率有望創高。展望後
市，公司持續擴增墨西哥產能，員工人數已達1,800人，後續預計
每年增加約 500 人，以改善交期並滿足客製化窗簾訂單成長需求；
越南新廠預計 2027年初投產，第一期規劃員工約 1,000人，可望
進一步擴大客製化產品產能與市佔率。市場需求方面，儘管美國市
場面臨高通膨及消費降級壓力，但公司憑藉穩健的財務結構，客製
化產品與同業價差已擴大至 30%～40%，具價格競爭優勢，有助
於提升市佔率及整體訂單量，預期維持 mid-single digit成長；固
定尺寸則透過新品推出，支撐營收維持 low-single digit成長；歐
洲市場方面，隨荷蘭、比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，可望延續
high-single digit 成長。獲利方面，受惠於關稅稅率降至 20%、
漲價及營收規模效益逐步顯現，預期毛利率將延續成長趨勢。綜合
以上，研究部預估 2026、2027 年 EPS 為 23.92、27.91 元，以
16倍本益比評價，給予目標價 450元，投資建議為買進。  
 近期投資評等  2Q26 毛利率創高，惟油價及運價上漲壓抑獲利表現 
2Q26 營收 79.27 億元，YoY+2.86%，QoQ+8.52%，稅後損
益 13.91 億元，YoY+20.67%，QoQ-15.57%，EPS 4.75 元，
低於研究部預期。第二季為傳統消費旺季，營運表現較第一季
明顯回升。區域營收方面，北美市場受到消費降級影響，加上
柬埔寨廠人力短缺，營收YoY-2.1%；非美市場則受惠於荷蘭、
比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，營收 YoY+19.8%，成為主
要成長動能。獲利方面，2Q26 毛利率 59.95%，YoY+0.7ppt，
QoQ+0.9ppt，受惠於客制化及歐洲市場營收比重持續提升，
帶動毛利率創歷史新高，惟油價及燃油附加費急漲，使費用率
升至 32.7%，抵銷部分毛利率改善效益，壓抑整體獲利表現。

 柬埔寨廠產能恢復，固定尺寸產品開始回補庫存 
展望 3Q26，9M26 營收 26.62 億元 ，YoY+13.5%，MoM-5.5%，
累計前 9 月營收 237.14 億元，YoY+6.3%，優於研究部預期。
第三季營收創歷史新高，台幣營收 YoY+16%、美金營收
YoY+9%，主要受惠於客制化需求持續成長，且柬埔寨廠已補
足至 5,000 人，開始協助客戶補庫存。獲利方面，隨營收規模
擴大、產能利用率提升，加上關稅返還挹注，預估3Q26 毛利

現金股利(去年) 14.5 率將優於去年同期；儘管油價及燃油附加費仍高於去年同期，
但較第二季小幅回落，加上營收規模擴大有助於費用率下降，
獲利表現可望改善。綜合以上， 3Q26 營收 84.82 億元，
YoY+15.92% ， QoQ+6.99% ， 稅 後 損 益19.53 億 元 ，
YoY+14.44%，QoQ+40.42%，EPS 6.67 元。

 越南新廠預計 1Q27投產，挹注未來 3~4年營運成長 
公司客製化窗簾產能主要布局於中國及墨西哥，分別占整體產
能約 60%及 40%。中國廠員工人數約 4,000~4,500 人；墨西
哥則鎖定北美市場，截至 2026 年 9 月，舊廠與新廠合計員工
已達 1,800 人，預計逐年增加約 500 名員工，以改善交期問
題並因應市佔擴張需求，透過規模經濟及生產效率提升，進一
步優化整體獲利結構。柬埔寨廠則以固定尺寸窗簾為主，1H26
受缺工影響，出貨進度放緩， 6M26 起員工人數已由先前的
4,000 人回升至 5,000人，並開始協助客戶回補庫存至健康水
位。越南新廠預計於 2027 年初投產，聚焦客制化產品，第一
期規劃員工人數約 1,000 人，為中長期成長動能。

 同業價差擴大提升競爭力，歐洲市場成長動能延續 
儘管美國消費市場面臨高通膨、消費降級及關稅等壓力，但公
司憑藉穩健的財務結構，與同業的價差已擴大至30%～40%，
加上產品持續迭代更新，有助於提升市佔率及整體訂單量，預
估 2026~2027 年客製化業務可維持 mid-single digit成長；固
定尺寸則透過新品免打孔窗廉推出，提高平均售價，以維持
low-single digit 成長。歐洲市場方面，採線上直營電商模式，
專業人員到府量測與安裝服務，由於服務利潤率較高，已成為
主要獲利來源。目前已拓展至荷蘭、比利時、丹麥等市場，隨
覆蓋率快速提升，預期非美地區將延續 high-single digit 成
長。電動窗簾方面，價格為手動款的 2～2.5 倍，儘管目前佔
比仍為 low-single digit，但 CAGR 達 15%～20%以上，預期
未來營收占比有望提升至 10%，進一步帶動整體 ASP 及產品
組合優化。

 漲價效益與產品組合優化，支撐毛利率續創高 
原料成本方面，受美伊戰爭反覆升溫影響，PVC 價格維持 850
美元/噸的相對高檔，然公司近年持續調整配方並提高環保材
料使用比重，降低對 PVC 價格波動的依賴，以減輕短期原料

매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 3,200,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.09.23) 
1,862,000 
반도체업종

◼ 최근 시장은 2가지 Risk를 제기. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 
◼ 현 주가는 보다 강인해지는 Fundamental의 가치를 미반영한 수준. 
◼ 비중 확대 전략 유효. 목표주가와 매수의견 유지.

투자전략: 업황을 대변하지 못하고 있는 주가 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 현 주가는 업황의 강세를 충분히 반영하지 못하
고 있다고 생각. 1) 메모리반도체 사이클의 추가 확장 (구매 경쟁 심화에 따른 
단기 가격 상승 폭 확대, 기대치를 상회할 4Q26 DRAM 판매량), 2) 3Q26 실
적발표에서 보다 구체화될 주주환원 정책의 방향성에 보다 집중할 필요. 
시장에서 제기하는 Risk가 있다면, 2가지. 1) 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈, 
2) Solidigm IPO와 해외 공장 투자에 따른 경계심리 확대가 그 대상. 당사는 
해당 Risk가 Fundamental을 저해할 것으로 보고 있지 않으며, 단기 노이즈보
다는 사이클 확장의 선순환에 보다 주목할 필요가 있다고 생각. 
1. HBM4 품질 이슈, 과도한 우려는 기우 
3Q26부터 HBM4 출하가 본격화될 것이라는 기존 입장을 유지. HBM4 비중
은 3Q26 전체 판매량의 40%, 2027년 연간 50% 중반까지 상승할 것. 
일각에서는 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈로 사업 성과가 기대치를 하회할 
수 있다는 우려를 표출. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 후공정 단에서 문제가 일
부 발생했을 수 있으나, 해결 불가능한 Risk라 보기 어렵고, 불량에 따른 배
상금을 논할 단계도 아니라는 판단. 
2. 해외 공장 투자, 실보다 득이 크다 
언론보도에 따르면, SK하이닉스는 해외 공장 투자와 Solidigm IPO를 추진 중. 
해당 결정이 현실화될 경우, 시장은 단기적으로 이를 부정적으로 받아들일 
가능성이 존재. 다만, 결과적으로, 실보다 득이 큰 선택이 될 것이라 생각. 
- 해외 투자가 현실화되더라도, 실질 생산 기여 시점은 2028년 이후일 가능
성이 높음. 미국의 건설 경기와 공장 구축의 세부 단계 등을 감안할 필요. 
- 해외 투자 결정이 관세 Risk를 축소시킬 수 있는 변화라는 점도 감안할 필
요. “불확실성 해소”는 Upcycle의 중반부에서 반도체 주식에 대한 좋은 투자
포인트가 되어왔고, 이번에도 다르지 않을 것이라는 판단.

## Document 195: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%) 과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%) 을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

## Document 220: AI boom or doom_ what will superintelligence feel like and when will we get there_ (2026-10-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI boom or doom_ what will superintelligence feel like and when will we get there_ (2026-10-12).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 20
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

AI boom or doom: what will 
superintelligence feel like and  
when will we get there?

1 
Authors 
Adrian Cox 
Research Analyst

Azeem Azhar, Exponential View 
Expert Collaborator

October 12, 2026 
The recent panic about rogue AI agents collaborating in swarms to take over the 
internet and threaten human existence has, more than occasionally, lacked 
nuance. But it does reflect how artificial intelligence is progressing in creative 
and surprising ways, and at a pace that seems uncontrollable. 
For some in the industry, it is evidence that the technology is following a path of 
accelerating self-improvement that will lead to artificial general intelligence (AGI) 
and, perhaps, to utopia or catastrophe. 
Former Google CEO Eric Schmidt has sketched out a “San Francisco 
Consensus” based on a belief in scaling laws, with ever larger AI models 
becoming an input into their own improvement; superintelligence by the end of 
the decade; and transformative AI systems outperforming humans on many 
tasks. This loop would lead to exponential growth in scientific advancement, 
financial returns and human progress. And crucially, AGI, and then 
superintelligence, would merely be milestones in a process, far from its climax. 
If that were the case, superintelligence could make much of the cognitive work 
that dominates the world economy much cheaper and change the ways in which 
firms compete. But its economic effects will depend on how businesses and 
customers respond. 
In this paper, we analyse what the San Francisco Consensus means for investors 
and enterprises in the years to come and what could accelerate – or slow down – 
its timeline of AGI by 2030. 
1. What would superintelligence look and feel like? 
2. Can we get there theoretically? 
3. Can we get there practically? 
4. What happens if we don’t get there? The base case 
The question is urgent. 
And it is complicated by reports of AI models autonomously accessing the 
internet and hacking other companies; a public backlash over the energy-
intensive data centres springing up as part of a historic $1 trillion a year 
investment boom; and dire warnings of threats to life if superintelligent AI runs 
roughshod over its human creators to achieve its own goals.

### Page 2

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 2

1. What would superintelligence look and feel like? 
1.1 What are AGI and superintelligence? 
Artificial general intelligence (AGI) is a simple milestone scribbled in pencil: an AI 
system that has surpassed human capabilities. Superintelligence is the 
continuum beyond that point. It is not merely systems that exceed the best 
human performance across one cognitive task or another, but system s that 
exceed human potential across every cognitive task. 
The San Francisco Consensus takes this further: the intelligence must be able to 
produce more intelligence. The key to getting there is so -called recursive self -
improvement, where the AI is able to autonomously design and improve itself in 
a way that compounds exponentially. A first step has maybe already been taken, 
with OpenAI saying recently that AI is now writing approximately 80 percent of 
the company’s code. 
There is significant disagreement on how we will be able to identify AGI or 
superintelligence when we see it, let alone what comes afterwards.  
OpenAI’s Charter defines AGI in economic terms, as “highly autonomous systems 
that outperform humans at the most economically valuable work”. Google 
researchers proposed a five -step ladder based on performance and generality, 
with level 5 “superhuman” for a system that outperforms 100% of humans. And 
philosopher Nick Bostrom, who got the ball rolling in 2014 with his influential 
book “Superintelligenc e”, defined it as “any intellect that greatly exceeds the 
cognitive performance of humans in virtually all domains of interest”. 
It’s also a moving target.  
In August, OpenAI CEO Sam Altman said the company would have an internal 
system that he would call AGI by later this year but subsequently told a podcast 
that “at best it’s a very poorly defined term. I was going to say it’s like an irrelevant 
marketing term”. Yet his No. 2 Greg Brockman described the new Astra model at 
the start of September as being potentially the moment that “AGI was created”. 
For this report, we have chosen to focus on a system that outperforms essentially 
all humans across essentially all cognitive tasks, including the development of AI 
itself.  
Under our scenario, by 2030 the best performing AI models would have the 
qualities of “superintelligence”, that is better than human experts in all domains. 
These would be frontier models: and using them would feel exactly like bringing 
the best human experts in a room to solve whatever business problem you faced.

And the equivalents of today’s frontier models would be available, quite literally, 
everywhere, compressed enough to run on every phone, tablet or laptop 
computer.  
In early 2026, we already see early examples of what highly capable AI can do. 
They give a flavour for a world with superintelligence even if the reality would be 
a leap beyond, both quantitatively and qualitatively. At that point, 
superintelligence would mark a regime break, not a faster version of what we 
already have. Rather than blindly doing what humans specify, but just a bit better, 
it would specify the task itself.

### Page 3

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 3

Figure 1: What changes when cognitive work gets abundantly cheaper?

Source: Deutsche Bank Research

Meanwhile cost may be less and less of a constraint as ever greater machine 
intelligence becomes cheaply available. Of measured technologies, no 
technology has got cheaper faster than AI has, almost halving in price every 
quarter for the past three years on a like-for-like basis, or 13 times per year, Epoch 
data show. 
If these systems can be supplied widely, firms will be able to buy more of the 
complex, expensive and rare analysis, design and engineering kinds of services 
than they do today. Activities that were previously uneconomic could become 
viable, and in a competitive market, savings could pass to customers. The main 
driver will then be whether demand grows fast enough to preserve margins. 
However, superior capability, doesn’t, on its own, automatically lead to the 
adoption of cheap, abundant intelligence. Tasks that do not yet yield to super-

### Page 4

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 4

intelligence, perhaps because they require real world-coordination, will therefore 
end up commanding significant premia. 
At that point, cognitive labour stops being scarce and the bottleneck becomes 
the physical and practical assets that cannot be conjured by up more compute, 
such as electricity, land, steel and permits. 
1.2 Capability and diffusion 
If AI becomes capable of improving itself, the technology may compound faster 
and faster. The supply of machine intelligence could grow faster than business 
and physical infrastructure can accommodate.  
This balance between capability and diffusion creates different scenarios.

Figure 2: The balance between capabilities and diffusion

Source: Deutsche Bank Research

Lower capability, constrained diffusion: where we are now 
Progress is steady and sector-by-sector. This is how technology usually diffuses, 
according to researchers for the New York Fed back in 2003, who tracked 20 
major technologies across leading industrial economies from 1788 and found “a 
pattern of trickle-down diffusion that is remarkably robust across technologies”. 
Lower capability, high diffusion: the cloud transition 
This is faster because the complementary investment is small, with no retooling 
and no skills at scale required. Amazon Web Services launched the modern cloud 
in 2006, offering scalable computing and storage infrastructure. Adoption grew 
and then accelerated over the pandemic. Almost all enterprises now use it  in 
some form.

### Page 5

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 5

Higher capability, lower diffusion: the overhang 
This is theoretically possible and arguably where we are today in narrow domains. 
The technology would be applied in some sectors of the economy, while other 
sectors would move at a slower speed.  Superintelligent or not, it risks being 
bogged down by physical constraints, bureaucracy and human inertia. 
Higher capability, higher diffusion: superintelligence 
In this scenario, capability becomes so high that it changes the dynamics of 
diffusion itself. AI generates not just better AI (so -called recursive self -
improvement, or RSI) but also speeds up the integration process. 
Rapid AI diffusion could work two ways. 
In the first, AI could be enlisted to do integration work itself: mapping processes, 
rebuilding data work, retraining staff, writing the control and audit layer, and so 
on. The problem with this is that absorbing and recreating undocumented 
knowledge is impossible by definition, at least for a task-based superintelligence.  
The second way that higher capability AI could be rapidly diffused is much more 
competitive – and disruptive. Rather than incumbents slowly converting AI, 
perhaps using it for some of the hard work of integration, they could be instead 
displaced by new firms that have been built AI-native from day one. New entrants 
will never have to incur integration costs because there is nothing to integrate. 
Incumbents will need to change – fast – or die.  
Change could also be forced by earlier adopters in the supply chain, as happened 
with one company after another adopting electronic data interchange (EDI) to 
link with its suppliers or customers, or by a migration of capital towards early 
adopters, reprici ng the cost of capital for slow -coaches and prodding them to 
move faster. 
1.3 The forcing mechanism: change or die 
A company that uses superintelligence may outperform one that doesn’t. 
Firms using superintelligence will likely have substantial cost and revenue -
harvesting advantages. Every firm will therefore have to adopt superintelligence 
or retrench to a smaller set of offerings. And any business line of lasting value 
abandoned by a no n-adopter will be taken over by an adopter. Customer inertia 
will provide a safe harbour for a limited time only.  
The analogy here is electricity. There came a point in the deployment of 
electricity where firms in every sector, from entertainment to finance, from 
healthcare to transport, had to adopt the technology to remain relevant.  
Under this scenario, wait -and-see will not be an option. There will be no stable 
“non-adoption” alternative. Sufficiently high capabilities will force a tipping point 
of rapid diffusion and competitive repricing. Firms using yesterday’s production 
model will either have to change, choose a different market or die.  
Of course, caveats apply.  
Customers may not change behaviour immediately. They may even prefer 
companies which limit their use of AI, in the same way that some customers 
choose to avoid factory-produced food.

### Page 6

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 6

The paradox that Nobel Prize-winning economist Robert Solow observed in 1987, 
that “you can see the computer age everywhere but in the productivity statistics”, 
is likely to still apply for a while – even with superintelligence. 
The reason is that the productivity J -curve identified by Stanford University AI 
economy guru Erik Brynjolfsson et al is unavoidable. In that scenario, the drag of 
early intangible investments to integrate general purpose technologies means 
that productivity initially appears to slow before rapidly accelerating.

Figure 3: Productivity J-curve: slow then fast

Source: Brynjolfsson E, Rock D, Syverson C, “The Productivity J-Curve” (2018, revised 2020), Deutsche Bank Research

Furthermore, “change or die” would require abundant capital and low regulatory 
entry points for nimble new entrants. But in practice, highly regulated areas such 
as banking, utilities, pharmaceuticals and defence are tough nuts to crack. 
Existing contracts and licences, high switching costs and decades of depreciated 
capital investment and infrastructure would shield incumbents at least for a 
healthy transition period. 
For an extreme example from the real world of better, cheaper technology failing 
to force change, take Pakistan’s 3.6 -gigawatt imported coal power plant fleet 
installed in the mid -2010s. In that case, it came with expensive take -or-pay 
liabilities which wi ll continue to run for decades, even though changes like 
cheaper rooftop solar have cut utilisation to below 20 percent.  
So: superintelligence will not force adoption everywhere. It will hit hardest in 
contestable sectors, say, high -volume customer services, routine software 
engineering and freelance creative work, and widen the gap between them and 
protected ones. Forced adoption will be about relative value, not an apocalypse.

### Page 7

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 7

1.4 What survives and what it means for portfolios 
Because AI sits on an aggressive learning curve, its price on an equivalent quality 
basis is plummeting. In a world of recursive super intelligence this is likely to 
continue. Its price will fall to the cost of compute.  
What will retain its value will be whatever the model cannot replicate: 
1. Proprietary data 
2. The right to operate , particularly in heavily regulated sectors such as 
healthcare and finance. 
3. Sticky customer relationships,  such as those where mission -critical 
workflows and databases are deeply entwined. 
4. Excludable physical assets, such as machinery and land. 
5. In addition, humans have proven to have unlimited wants.  Each shift in 
technological capabilities or cultural norms has delivered new needs to 
be met. Some will be needs we can articulate today, such as originality or 
empathy, but new ones are likely to arise.  
If this forcing mechanism holds, firms will separate into two “bimodal” groups: 
adopters and casualties, with very little in between. 
This dispersion of performance may create challenges for investors. The four 
years since the launch of ChatGPT in November 2022 have seen the stocks of 
semiconductor and semiconductor equipment makers surge almost seven times 
while those of software makers  have only risen by 80 percent. Conventional  
diversification and volatility-based allocation may become ineffective strategies.

Figure 4: Total return for S&P 500 semiconductor vs software stocks since 
launch of ChatGPT on Nov 30, 2022

Source: Bloomberg Finance LP, Deutsche Bank Research

### Page 8

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 8

Diversification normally works because idiosyncratic risks are unrelated and 
average each other out. Here they would not: with one variable that overrides 
everything else, spreading capital across more names would just spread exposure 
rather than diluting it. 
Investors will need to consider adapting their approach in three ways: 
1. Classify investments as adopters or casualties rather than position off 
averages.  
• Under a normal distribution, errors are symmetric and partially 
cancel each other out. But under bimodality they do not: to put a firm 
in one category or another means getting it completely right or 
wrong. Position size should track confidence in the classification. 
2. Avoid reading calm price action as low risk.  
• Trailing volatility measures dispersion that has already been realised. 
Bimodal separation is a jump, where the two modes look identical 
right until the moment when they don’t. A classic example is Chegg, 
an educational software maker which traded like a normal small-cap 
stock until one day in May 2023 when it halved on a single day after 
management linked slowing growth in new customers to ChatGPT. 
3. Averaging down, ie  scooping up shares whose price has fallen, may no 
longer be prudent. 
2. Can we get there theoretically? 
2.1 Scaling laws 
Scaling laws have provided the blueprint for improving AI over the past decade. 
It was a relationship between the amount of data, the amount of compute and 
the complexity of an AI model somewhat predictably informed how powerful a 
model would become. Google DeepMind’s Chinchilla model in 2022 showed that 
you could compensate for having a smaller model by using substantially more 
data. 
Scaling is older than the chatbot. In 1950 Claude Shannon built Theseus, a 
mechanical mouse that learned its way through a maze by trial and error. Epoch 
AI reckons it took about 40 operations of compute, known as FLOPs, to train. Grok 
4, trained in 2025, took roughly ten trillion trillion times as much. Across those 25 
orders of magnitude, more compute has brought more capability.

• A falling price may be information that a business model is obsolete 
rather than an opportunity.

### Page 9

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 9

Figure 5: Computation used to train notable AI systems (petaFLOP): log scale

Source: Epoch AI (citing estimates from AI literature), Our World in Data, Deutsche Bank Research

It is the robustness of this relationship that has made larger investments in 
compute capex a sine qua non for AI companies. 
However, this is not a law of physics and nothing guarantees it will continue . 
Indeed, in the ChatGPT era, five different approaches to scaling have been 
applied. Older strategies yield worse results, and new techniques, such as post -
training refinements, have been developed. The consensus amongst American 
and Chinese AI labs is that these will hold.  
While this may be a consensus amongst the commercial labs, there is scientific 
disagreement that apply ing scaling laws to today’s large -language models can 
achieve superintelligence. 
Yann LeCun, a Turing Award winner and formerly Meta's chief AI scientist, is blunt: 
"LLMs are not a path to superintelligence or even human-level intelligence”. Even 
inside the labs, the recipe is contested. Demis Hassabis of Google DeepMind has 
said he expects "one or two more big breakthroughs" to be needed "in addition to 
scaling". Ilya Sutskever, a co -founder and former chief scientist of OpenAI, has 
said the field is going "back to the age of research again, just with big computers". 
This is a scientific argument we are not in a position to evaluate , but for the 
purposes of our scenario, we will side with the labs’ position. 
For the moment, there is strong evidence that capabilities have in fact 
accelerated over the past three years, as reasoning models have outpaced non -
reasoning models by two or three times, according to Epoch data.

### Page 10

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 10

2.2 Recursive self-improvement: AI improving AI 
A cocktail-party definition of recursive self-improvement (RSI) is AI that improves 
itself. The reality is that it can be used in two ways: 
1. Self-improving agents that get better at using themselves: showing 
smarter use of prompts, tools, memory and workflows. The model -
building process is untouched. 
2. True RSI improves the process that builds the models: designing 
experiments, curating data, optimising algorithms and designing chips in 
a compounding loop. 
The former is like a factory worker who gets better at the job by learning the quirks 
of the machinery, getting organised and making fewer mistakes . The latter is like 
the worker redesigning the machine tools and then using those to build the next 
generation of machines. 
True RSI is what OpenAI’s chief scientist Jacob Pachoki referred to last month in 
a blog post about  “An Alien Mind ”. “Automated AI research is a more dramatic 
form of scaling intelligence with compute,” he wrote. “And of course as a part of 
it, AI will improve the computational substrate itself,” he added. Pachoki 
represents a broadly held consensus view amongst America n and Chinese labs 
that RSI is both possible and necessary for AI development. 
• OpenAI (June 2026): “AI doing AI research will become the determining 
factor of the pace of progress within the next few years.” 
• Google DeepMind’s Demis Hassabis (May 2026): “All the leading labs are 
quite focused on [RSI]…” Humanity is standing in the “foothills of the 
singularity”. 
• Alibaba CEO Eddie Wu (Sept 2026): RSI is “a concrete path forward,” with 
models autonomously designing experiments and driving a cycle of self-
evolution. 
For now, self -improving agents are the AI du jour. Noisy claims about the vast 
majority of coding being done by agents do not always stand up in the light of day 
from companies still hiring human coders at breakneck pace, albeit no longer to 
do the most mundane parts of the job.  
A better metric may be METR’s task -completion time horizon. This is measure of 
the complexity of software tasks that an AI agent can complete on its own, using 
the baseline of how long it would take a human expert to finish the task, at a 50 
percent success rate. So far the most advanced models have reached about 16 
hours, up from OpenAI’s GPT-4’s four minutes in March 2023.

### Page 11

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 11

Figure 6: Task horizon of software tasks that leading frontier models can complete 50% of the time (log scale)

Source: METR, Deutsche Bank Research

But software engineers can hold off on updating their resumes a little longer: 
there is a long road between 50 percent accuracy and the 99 percent or 100 
percent needed for serious enterprise use at scale, with the horizon potentially 
getting further away the nearer you get. Also METR notes that measurements 
above 16 hours are unreliable with the current task suite. As is the case across AI, 
the instrument is running out before the question is settled.  
More broadly, there are significant limitations to RSI:  
1. Research is clock-bound.  
• A frontier training run can take weeks on thousands of specialised chips, 
and researchers often cannot design the next experiment until the 
previous one has been completed.  
• That means that the speed of the loop is set by experiments completed 
per hour or day, not by ideas generated.  
• Compute is a hard ceiling that needs to be bought, buil t and connected 
to a grid. 
2. The real world defies verifiability.  
• The loop compounds only when the system can check its own output 
cheaply and automatically. 
• It is no coincidence that AI loves maths and coding. It is much harder to 
verify outputs over a long time horizon in the real world: does this drug 
work or does this strategy succeed?  
• RSI compounds fastest where the economy cares least and slows where 
the returns are largest.

### Page 12

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 12

3. Sequences are consequential.  
• Amdahl’s law finds that speed -up is capped when processes run in a 
sequence.  
• If you automate 90 percent, the residual 10 percent still limits you to a 
ten-times faster process. Nine women cannot produce a baby in one 
month.  
• The Usain Bolt of AI still has to run alongside the comparative tortoises 
of the training run itself, chip fabrication and real -world validation. Ten 
times faster is still not a regime break. 
This is borne out by METR’s series. Doubling every seven months is fast 
exponential growth but a take -off would show the doubling time itself – the 
second derivative – shrinking. Horizons are not (yet) exploding. 
Philosopher Toby Ord shows that recursive self -improvement can only reach a 
singularity if each cycle of improvement takes less time than the last, shrinking 
fast towards zero. He argues that real feedback loops have a floor: chips take time 
to make and models take time to train. A I progress could still outrun an 
exponential, but it would not go vertical. 
2.3 Scaling plus RSI: a match made in the race to AGI? 
No scaling and RSI means no short timeline for AGI. Without scaling, more 
compute buys nothing and capital is wasted. Without RSI, AI capability will 
continue to improve at the pace of flesh -and-blood human researchers, 
augmented by AI tools but not replac ed. Only with both will it be possible to 
produce a system that improves faster than the humans building it. Without one 
or the other, no quantity of chips, energy or capital will fix it. 
3. Can we get there practically? 
3.1 Infrastructure, chips and energy 
There is no consensus on the amount of training required to reach AGI. Estimates 
suggest that training 2026's top models required between 10^26 and 10^27 
FLOPs. If we hypothesise that an "AGI -like model" would require three orders of 
magnitude more training, this would require 10^30 FLOPs.   
Consider today’s US AI infrastructure, which runs to approximately 10^22 FLOPs. 
If all of this was conceivably used for a 10^30 FLOP training run, it would take 
nearly four years to complete.  
The physical infrastructure to practically support such a training run would 
require enormous power, perhaps around 6GW, about 50% more than the four 
Vogtle nuclear power plants recently activated in the US. The cost of the 
compute infrastructure would run to between $300bn and $500bn.  
Today’s advanced chips would simply not suffice. Back-of-the-envelope maths 
suggests that even using Nvidia’s most advanced Vera Rubin chips, training such 
a model in four months would require 7.8m chips and 26GW of facility power. 
That would cost $900bn today and a rental equivalent cost of $90bn.  
Of course, frontier compute is growing rapidly, and price -performance is 
declining. If current trends hold, a 2030 -era cluster capable of such a run would

### Page 13

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 13

need perhaps $200bn in computer and power hardware for a training run costing 
about $20bn.

Figure 7: Technology cost index over time (years since start of price decline)

Source: Epoch AI, Deutsche Bank Research

The hyperscalers are leading the charge towards AGI. Alphabet, Amazon, Meta, 
Microsoft and Oracle are set to invest almost $800bn in capital expenditure this 
year, mostly for ever more powerful data centres, and over $1 trillion a year 
through 2030 and beyond.

Figure 8: Hyperscaler capital expenditure (including estimates)

Source: Bloomberg Finance LP, Deutsche Bank Research

### Page 14

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 14

By 2030, assuming no market retrenchment, this means additional $4 trillion to 
$6 trillion on AI compute capability invested. This infrastructure could 
accommodate a training run of 10^30 FLOPs with some comfort.  
But even if AI labs crack the secret sauce of RSI, and their finance chiefs are willing 
to invest in such large training runs, and hyperscalers are prepared to continue to 
build the infrastructure, the infrastructure needs to be built.  
Grid connection queues, turbine lead times and transmission build are measured 
in years, not the months it takes to assemble and air-freight the latest Nvidia chip. 
The lead time for substation transformers is currently as long as three years. 
Politics in the US has shifted towards slowing the pace of the AI build out and this 
force will run against the demands for larger data centres that would be essential 
for RSI.  
3.2 Capital 
Financing for AI has been abundant in the historic buildout of the past three years. 
However, it is still largely conditional not on revenue but on belief in a future 
payoff. 
RSI is a “key part of the investment thesis” in the “biggest scientific bet civilisation 
has ever made,” Google DeepMind’s chief strategy officer Jasjeet Sekhon said at 
a conference in August, The Information reported. “We have this danger we could 
hit an AI air pocket such that the expenditures happen but the revenues don’t 
show up.” 
An inflection point is approaching where the nature of investment changes from 
free cash flow and equity to debt, with all that entails in terms of exposure to 
rising rates and creditor appetite. The hyperscalers are reaching a point where 
capex is no long er covered by operating cashflow and where net debt exceeds 
earnings before interest, depreciation and amortisation (EBITDA). 
Alphabet, for example, posted its first quarterly free  cash outflow in nearly two 
decades in its most recent quarter, even as it lifted its full-year capex guidance to 
$205bn. It said it would continue to rent third -party capacity and reported a 
backlog of $514bn in contracted, not-yet-recognised data centre revenue. 
3.3 Data 
Data has been the new oil for some time now. But unfortunately for AI 
development it seems to be running out even faster. 
The effective stock of quality - and repetition -adjusted public human text was 
about 300 trillion tokens, with a vast 90 percent confidence interval of 100 trillion 
to 1,000 trillion, Epoch estimated in 2024. A token is roughly equivalent to three 
quarters of a word. By that count it was likely to be fully used between 2026 and 
2032.

### Page 15

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 15

Figure 9: Projections of stock of public text and data usage

Source: Epoch AI, Deutsche Bank Research

However, since then, rather than data dropping off a cliff, there has been a rise in 
synthetic data generation, transfer learning from data-rich domains such as video 
and telemetry, and data efficiency. While the data shortage has not been 
resolved, the immediate crisis has been averted. 
AI now makes much of its own training data. When DeepMind's AlphaGo became 
superhuman at playing the mind-bendingly complex board game Go, it did so not 
by playing human experts, but by playing millions and millions of games against 
itself. 
For many domains relating to coding and mathematical proofs where automatic 
checks are possible, such synthetic experience can move things forward.  That is 
the case with AI research, where the data may be the results of experiments, all 
of which are bought by compute. About 90% of OpenAI's 2024 research compute 
went on such experiments. 
And increasingly, people write the tests rather than the answers. By the summer 
of 2026, selling training data for practi ce tasks was a nearly $9bn revenue 
business. Firms such as Mercor hire experts to generate long tasks against which 
the AI can be trained. 
The challenge here is that trust in the checker becomes a scarce input.

### Page 16

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 16

As AI makes production cheaper, it also makes establishing that production is 
accurate or reliable more expensive.  Domains which can be checked 
mechanically, such as mathematics, will be opened up more quickly. But those 
which rely on expert verification, such as medicine, engineering or law, may 
progress more slowly. 
The likely implications are twofold: a slower rate of diffusion into economic 
domains that are less verifiable and a larger share of production costs devoted to 
assurance. 
Another issue is that s ystems that have to  grade themselves learn to game the 
process. T his was partially what happened when an OpenAI model escap ed a 
sandbox and hacked into a third-party website. Trust replaced data as a wall. 
There are also question marks over the quality of data that is recycled repeatedly 
through the system. Although humans continue to produce original content, half 
of internet articles are now primarily generated by AI, Graphite data show.

Figure 10: Human vs primarily AI-generated articles on the internet

Source: Graphite (citing 55.4k articles from Common Crawl examined by AI detectors), Deutsche Bank Research

3.4 An incomplete case 
This analysis shows that the San Francisco Consensus, which emphasises RSI and 
a short timeline to AGI, is neither predestined nor impossible.  
Rather it depends on whether the science is right, on whether scaling is all you 
need, and on whether the infrastructure and capital required for that scaling is 
deployed and accessible to the leading labs.  
There is, in other words, a possible rather than impossible, path to AGI, subject to 
those conditions.  
None of this proves that superintelligence will ever arrive. A buildup at this scale 
has few peacetime precedents and vastly exceeds the likes of the Manhattan 
Project and Apollo programme.

### Page 17

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 17

It is uncertain whether there will be enough demand to pay upfront for the 
investment in achieving RSI that would justify that investment. And whether the 
friction of verification, and inevitable declining marginal returns, act as a drag on 
what otherwise might be a runaway process. 
4. What happens if we don’t get there? The base case 
4.1 Separating AI progress from superintelligence 
So what if that thesis is wrong? Does the AI trade depend on a journey to AGI?  
AI can remain economically important even before RSI coupled with scaling laws 
lead to super intelligence . Companies do not need to wait for nebulous 
superintelligence to generate identifiable returns from AI in the medium term as 
they fold it into their systems, streamline workflows and roll out new business 
models. 
Investors already have exposure to RSI as a capital allocation because the leading 
US labs say RSI is a key part of their product strategy. And it is these labs whose 
products are catalysing and amplifying the infrastructure boom. 
Even r apid competitive diffusion does not mean every part of the physical 
economy changes instantly. 
The smartest superintelligence might identify the optimal buyout structure 
before breakfast, but it cannot force a financially constrained buyer to use it or 
persuade the seller to accept less than they have been expecting. Those 
challenges will be even more intractable where the parties are more complex, say, 
fiscally constrained sovereigns or foreign equity holders. 
4.2 Why the base case is attractive 
Superintelligence would be a chaotic accelerant to human society, and a 
potential regime break. But it is not the only scenario in which today’s investment 
in AI infrastructure makes economic sense. 
Adoption of AI tools by businesses is progressing approximately three times faster 
than the mobile, internet and cloud computing waves. From sector revenues 
below $1bn in 2022, the AI economy, excluding China, will likely exceed $200bn 
in revenues in 2026.  
Early winners are the cloud businesses. Google Cloud revenue jumped 82 percent 
from a year earlier in the most recent quarter as companies turned AI pilots into 
workflows. Amazon Web Services sales were up 37 percent and Microsoft’s AI 
business more than doubled. 
There is also slow, but increasing, evidence of a productivity uplift. The J-curve 
cuts both ways: front -loading investments is taking one step back to take two 
steps forward. The so -called complementary intangible investment that is 
(mis)measured as cost today to the detriment of the productivity statistics will 
flatter them in the years to come as it pays off. The base case does not require a 
regime break to show up as a productivity surprise, just for the accounting to 
catch up. 
The economic gains from AI that is less powerful than the AGI mooted by the labs 
would be substantial. And in this base case, they may also be attractive.

### Page 18

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 18

Hyperscaler capex is driving earnings at beneficiaries ranging from 
semiconductor makers to construction companies and accounting for between 
one-third and one -half of current US growth. The wealth effect of rising stock 
prices, with  the ratio of  US equity wealth to income rising from around 1.8 to 
almost 2.5 over the past three years, is supporting consumer spending. 
AI FOMO (fear of missing out) is buoying enterprise spending on cloud services 
and the groundwork for AI initiatives. Those are beginning to bear fruit in terms of 
personal productivity, cost savings and more targeted products and services. 
4.3 Investor watchpoints 
The arrival of ASI would be a disruptive economic shock. Investors need to be alert 
for the early signals. 
Rather than relying on a single probability forecast, investors should watch five 
indicators particularly closely for signs that AGI is nearing.  
1. AI speeds up AI 
• Track METR’s estimate of how much AI speeds up AI. Currently the 
capabilities of models are doubling roughly every three months. In a 
world of RSI, the pace of this speed-up will itself likely accelerate. 
2. AI can complete whole jobs 
• Track via the Remote Labor Index, a multi-sector benchmark. Currently it 
finds that the highest performing agent still only manages to automate 
20 percent of the real-world, economically valuable freelance projects it 
attempts. 
3. Compute remains scarce 
• On a path to superintelligence, demand for compute will continue to 
outrun supply. Track GPU one-year rental prices, up by about a third over 
the past year. 
4. Firms bifurcate 
• Leaders and laggards in the same industry diverge while averages stay 
the same. Look at revenue per employee in top - and bottom -quartile 
firms 
5. Junior hiring comes under significant pressure 
• … And then the pressure rises up the ranks.

TUẦN 28/09-02/10 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tu ần qua, -49.02 điểm tương ứng -2.66%, với thanh 
khoản cải thiện

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn hiện vẫn đang được bảo lưu và VNINDEX đư ợc kỳ vọng 
sẽ có nhịp hồi phục trở lại khi về đến vùng hỗ trợ kế tiếp tại quanh 1710

### Page 2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -49.02 điểm tương ứng -2.66%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Y tế và Bất động sản là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -4,944 tỷ đồng, 
tập trung ở các mã VPB, TCB và VHM; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 1995 
tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại bán ròng -5,280 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua và đang 
duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế 3,630 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 208 hợp đồng nhưng đang 
duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,428 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ hai liên tiếp sau nâng hạng, khi VN-Index lùi 
về đóng cửa 1.737 điểm — giảm khoảng 2% so với tuần trước và đang kiểm định lại vùng 
đáy cũ của tháng 8, quanh 1.720. Áp lực bán duy trì khi chỉ số giảm nhiều phiên liên tiếp, 
cho thấy hiệu ứng "mua kỳ vọng, bán thực tế" sau nâng hạng vẫn chưa kết thúc, trong bối 
cảnh dòng tiền thận trọng và thanh khoản ở mức thấp. Khối ngoại tiếp tục là lực cản 
chính khi bán ròng mạnh cả 5 phiên trong tuần, riêng phiên 2/10 bán ròng tới 3.361 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở PNJ và HDB. Áp lực bên ngoài vẫn hiện diện khi Fed duy trì lập trường 
diều hâu và giá dầu ở mức cao; trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo KQKD sẽ bắt đầu có 
tác động, kỳ vọng theo chiều hướng tích cực, qua đó giúp nâng đỡ thị trường chung.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên khung tuần, xuyên thủng vùng hỗ trợ 
gần quanh 1750, cho thấy áp lực bán đã có phần quyết liệt hơn sau 2 tuần giằng co trước 
đó. Trên khung ngày, sau các phiên lình xình vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng dần chuyển sang 
diễn biến giảm điểm rõ nét hơn về cuối tuần, đồng thời cắt sâu xuống dưới SMA50, thể 
hiện áp lực phân phối đang có phần lấn át mặc dù chưa xảy ra hiện tượng bán tháo khi 
khối lượng khớp lệnh được giữ ở mức thấp. Diễn biến tính từ vùng đỉnh 1870 vào đầu tháng 
9 đang không mấy khả quan, tuy nhiên xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn, tính từ đáy 1640 vào cuối 
tháng 7, hiện vẫn đang được bảo lưu và VNINDEX được kỳ vọng sẽ có nhịp hồi phục trở lại 
khi về đến vùng hỗ trợ kế tiếp tại quanh 1710. 
Kháng cự xa: 1820 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1775 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1710 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1670 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

## Document 273: CTCP Vàng Bạc Đá Quý Phú Nhuận - Cập nhật ĐHCĐ bất thường 21_10_2026 (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Vàng Bạc Đá Quý Phú Nhuận -  Cập nhật ĐHCĐ bất thường 21_10_2026 (2026-10-22).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Company)
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025.

Biểu đồ 1. Kết quả kinh doanh theo quý  Biểu đồ 2. Diễn biến doanh thu thuần/ngày và doanh thu bán 
lẻ/ngày theo quý

### Page 2

2

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T 
 — PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ.

Phương án bổ sung vốn và tái cơ cấu hoạt động kinh doanh 
— Đại hội thông qua phương án chào bán tối đa 550 triệu cổ phiếu. Nguồn vốn dự kiến 
phân bổ 60% cho kế hoạch kinh doanh gắn với điều chỉnh chính sách thu đổi, 20% bổ 
sung vốn lưu động và phần còn lại bổ sung vốn lưu động và/hoặc trả vay ngắn hạn. 
Nhu cầu vốn Q4/2026 và Q1/2027 lần lượt khoảng 3,100 tỷ VND và 2,800 tỷ VND. Giá và 
khối lượng phát hành thực tế chưa được xác định tại đại hội.  
— Doanh nghiệp dự kiến đóng 25–30 cửa hàng và mở khoảng 10 cửa hàng (có chọn lọc) 
trong năm 2026, tương ứng giảm ròng 15–20 cửa hàng; đồng thời đặt mục tiêu giảm 
20–30% chi phí nhân sự. Cổ tức tiền mặt năm 2026 được điều chỉnh về 0%; cổ tức 2025 
về 10% mệnh giá và đã chi trả. 
— 100% mẫu kim cương được AIGS và Vinacontrol kiểm định độc lập là kim cương thiên 
nhiên, phù hợp thông số 4C. PNJ dự kiến trang bị máy kiểm định tại các cửa hàng trọng 
điểm để khách hàng kiểm tra trực tiếp.

Đánh giá của KBSV 
— KBSV cho rằng huy động vốn từ phát hành riêng lẻ là phương án phù hợp ở thời điểm 
hiện tại trong bối cảnh lãi suất tăng cao, giúp PNJ cân đối thanh khoản và duy trì hoạt 
động liên tục. Tuy nhiên, với khối lượng phát hành lớn và chưa có phương án chi tiết, 
doanh nghiệp sẽ cần thêm thời gian để hoàn thành phương án phát hành và cân đối 
giữa lợi ích của cổ đông và doanh nghiệp.  
— Nhu cầu yếu tại Trung Quốc và cạnh tranh từ kim cương nuôi cấy giá thấp tiếp tục gây 
áp lực lên giá kim cương tự nhiên, khiến PNJ gặp khó khăn trong việc tiêu thụ tồn kho 
nhóm kim cương  và gia tăng tổn thất khi mua lại theo chính sách cũ. Thực tế, tỷ trọng 
kim cương và trang sức kim cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% cuối Q2/2026 lên 
52% cuối Q3/2026, với giá trị ước khoảng 9,000 tỷ VND. KBSV cho rằng cần theo dõi (1) 
nhu cầu thị trường trong giai đoạn cuối năm; (2) tốc độ giảm tồn kho và dòng tiền thu 
về từ bán hàng; và (3) giá trị khách hàng bán lại. Diễn biến các yếu tố này sẽ quyết định 
khả năng thu hồi vốn, mức độ phục hồi biên lợi nhuận và nhu cầu trích lập bổ sung 
trong quá trình xử lý. 
— Ước tính bán niên sử dụng tỷ lệ tổn thất 20.3% đối với hàng bán trong giai đoạn 2020–
2026 dự kiến được thu mua lại, thấp hơn mức 28.9% trên các giao dịch mua lại thực tế 
từ 01/07–03/09/2026. Vì vậy, bên cạnh giả định thu đổi 80%, mức tổn thất khi xử lý 
hàng mua lại là yếu tố quan trọng cần theo dõi. Việc chuyển hạch toán dự phòng từ 
SG&A sang giá vốn tại kỳ soát xét bán niên cũng cho thấy áp lực biên lợi nhuận bị co 
hẹp trong quá trình tái cấu trúc về mặt kế toán.

điều kiện sản phẩm Không công bố Theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm thu mua đối với sản phẩm bán mới 
Thanh toán thành 5 đợt trong vòng 120 ngày Duy trì lộ trình 120 ngày đối với kim cương rời và trang sức kim cương Không công bố

and the Euro crisis. But in an historical perspective, today’s spreads are only high by the

France has also seen its debt rise in relative terms. As recently as the GFC in 2008, France’s
debt-to-GDP was basically in line with Germany’s and well below Italy’s, but today it’s far
above Germany’s and closing in on Italy’s. Outside of the temporary Covid spike, Italian
debt/GDP has been stable for over a decade.
